# Personal Wallpaper Collection

Welcome to my personal collection of wallpapers! 

I have gathered these over time from various GitHub repositories, forums, and other corners of the internet to use on my own setups. 

### Disclaimer
**I do not own any of these images.** All artwork, photographs, and designs belong to their respective original creators and artists. 

Feel free to browse around, download your favorites, and use them for your own screens!
